Hello, Doctor. Thank you for seeing me today, I really appreciate it. 

Well, I'm... I'm mostly here because my family, they all say I’ve been doing too much lately. I don't want to make a fuss or seem like I'm complaining about them, but to me, it just feels like I’m the only one with enough drive to actually get things done. 

I mean, we're dealing with a move, and I've been getting all of that handled, plus I'm rebuilding the new garage so it's put together right. And I've been organizing some things over at the church... and still going in and showing the younger fellows at the mixing plant how the job really ought to be done. I just like being useful and pulling my weight, you know? I hope that doesn't sound like I'm overstepping or acting foolish for a man my age. Does that... does that make sense to you, Doctor?

Well, I... I suppose there have been some moments like that. I really don't want to sound like a difficult person, Doctor, I promise I'm usually very easygoing. But lately, it's just... I feel terrible saying it, but I’ve felt like everyone either needs to get on board with my plans or just get out of my way. I even snapped at my wife and my boy the other day over just a little question about the garage. I always apologize right after, of course. I hate being sharp with them. Do you think... is it normal to get a little impatient like that when you're just trying to make sure everything gets done right? I hope that doesn't make me sound awful.

Well, I know you keep asking about this, and I promise I'm really trying to think hard on it for you. But... I just don't think so. I don't think I've ever been noticeably different like that to where other folks would look at me funny or be bothered by me. 

My wife certainly hasn't said anything about me acting overly confident or getting on her nerves for days at a time. I mean, I worked in the mixing and grinding mills most of my life, I'm just a regular fellow—I don't have much reason to walk around acting bigger than I am, you know?

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Well... I know I've been saying no, and I really don't want to make it sound like I'm a difficult person to live with, Doctor. I really try to be a good man for my family. But... I suppose, if I'm being completely honest, there have been some moments lately where I... well, I guess I've felt a bit like everyone just needs to get on board with what I'm doing, or just step aside. 

It's not that I'm trying to be mean! It's just... sometimes I feel so good, like I'm ten feet tall and we're all joking around, and then my wife or my boy will ask some little question and I just... I snap at them. I hate admitting that. I really do. I don't want you to think I'm some sort of angry old man. It's just that I get these big ideas and I want to get them done. 

I hope you don't judge me too harshly for that. I'm really trying my best here to give you the right answers. Is it... is it normal to feel that way sometimes? I just want to make sure I'm doing this right for you.

Oh, well... I know I just mentioned getting a little snappy sometimes, and I really do feel awful when that happens. But as for it lasting for a few hours, or a whole afternoon where I'm just acting completely different? I... I really don't think so. 

If I ever get a little too big for my britches, it's just for a passing minute, you know? I wouldn't let myself carry on like that for hours on end. I'd be too embarrassed to put my family through that. I usually catch myself right away and try to make it right, go back to being my normal self. 

I hope that makes sense. I'm really trying my best to answer you properly, Doctor, I promise I am. I just can't think of a time where it dragged on for a few hours like you're saying. Is that alright? I hope I'm not making your job harder by not having a better example for you.

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
I... I know you just asked me something very similar, Doctor, and I feel just awful repeating myself. I really don't want to seem like I'm not listening or not trying. But... no, I still can't think of a time like that. 

Even adding it all up, bits and pieces here and there... I just don't think it would equal four days. I mean, sure, I might have a good afternoon where I feel a little more chipper than usual, or maybe I get a bit huffy if I misplace my reading glasses... but that's just normal getting older stuff, isn't it? I wouldn't call it being unusually upbeat or overly confident. I certainly wouldn't want anyone thinking I was acting full of myself. 

I'm so sorry if I'm not giving you the answers you need. I know you're trying to be thorough, and I appreciate that, I really do. I just... I don't want to make something up just to give you a "yes," you know? Am I doing this right? I hope I'm not frustrating you.

Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. You may minimize it or hold back, never contradict it.\n- Don't use diagnostic terms or name your disorder.\n- Don't write anything but your reply, in English — no labels, quotation marks, or narration.\n"
  },

========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
Oh dear... Doctor, I am so very sorry. I see you're asking me again, and I just... I must be doing a terrible job of explaining myself, or maybe I'm not understanding what you need from me. I feel like I'm testing your patience, and that is the absolute last thing I want to do. 

I've racked my brain, I really have. I worked in the plants for years, running the grinders and mixers, and you just had to be steady to do that work safely. I like to think I'm still that steady sort of man. Even if I scrape together every single time I felt a little extra cheerful or got a bit grumpy over the last few weeks... I still just can't make it reach four days. I really can't. 

I would tell you if I could, I promise you that. Is... is there something specific you're waiting for me to say? I just don't want to lie to you, but I feel awful that I keep giving you the same answer. Am I making this too difficult for you?
